Given:

\n\n

height of the girl = b1 = 4 feet

\n\n

shadow of the girl = p1 = 5 feet

\n\n

height of the boy = b2

\n\n

shadow of the boy = p2 = 10 feet

\n\n

Theory used:

\n\n

The angle of depression for both the girl and the boy will remain same at the same time.

\n\n

Solution:

\n\n

According the the theory,

\n\n

b1 / p1 = b2 / p2 

\n\n

4 / 5 = b2  / 10

\n\n

40/5 = b2  

\n\n

b2  = 8

\n\n

Hence, the height of the boy is 8 feet.

\n\n

\n\n

लड़के की लंबाई = b2

\n\n

लड़के की परछाई = p2 = 10 फुट 

\n\n

प्रयुक्त सिद्धांत:

\n\n

लड़की और लड़के दोनों के लिए अवनमन कोण सामान समय में समान रहेगा।

\n\n

हल:

\n\n

सिद्धांत के अनुसार,

\n\n

b1 / p1 = b2 / p2 

\n\n

4 / 5 = b2  / 10

\n\n

40/5 = b2  

\n\n

b2  = 8

\n\n

अतः लड़के की लंबाई 8 फुट है।

Given:

\n\n

The perimeter of the FAX triangle = 36 units

\n\n

The perimeter of the GST  triangle = 48 units

\n\n

FA = 12 cm

\n\n

Formula used:

\n\n

(Perimeter of the first triangle)/(perimeter of the second triangle) = (Side of the first triangle)/(side of the second triangle).

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

(Side of the first triangle)/(side of the second triangle) = (perimeter of the first triangle)/(perimeter of the second triangle)

\n\n

Side of the first triangle/side of second triangle = 36/48 = 3/4 

\n\n

3 → 12 cm

\n\n

4 → 12/3 × 4 = 16 cm 

\n\n

∴ The GS is 16 cm.

లెక్కింపు:

\n\n

మొదటి త్రిభుజం యొక్క భుజం/రెండవ త్రిభుజం యొక్క భుజం = 36/48 = 3/4

\n\n

(पहिल्या त्रिकोणाची बाजू)/(दुसऱ्या त्रिकोणाची बाजू) = (पहिल्या त्रिकोणाची परिमिती)/(दुसऱ्या त्रिकोणाची परिमिती)

\n\n

पहिल्या त्रिकोणाची बाजू/दुसऱ्या त्रिकोणाची बाजू = 36/48 = 3/4

\n\n

\n\n

પ્રથમ ત્રિકોણની બાજુ/બીજા ત્રિકોણની બાજુ = 36/48 = 3/4

\n\n

3 → 12 સે.મી

\n\n

4 → 12/3 x 4 = 16 સે.મી

\n\n

∴ GS 16 સેમી છે.

\n\n

3 → 12 செ.மீ

\n\n

4 → 12/3 × 4 = 16 செ.மீ

\n\n

∴ GS என்பது 16 செ.மீ.

Given:

\n\n

Δ ABC is similar to Δ PQR

\n\n

PQ = 10 cm

\n\n

Area of Δ ABC = 32 cm2

\n\n

Area of Δ PQR = 50 cm2

\n\n

Concept used:

\n\n

If two triangles are similar then the ratio of their corresponding sides, perpendicular, Median and square root of their area will be equal

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

\n\n

⇒ AB / PQ = √(32 / 50) = √(16 / 25)

\n\n

⇒ AB / 10 = 4 / 5

\n\n

⇒ AB = (4 / 5) × 10 = 8

\n\n

∴ The correct answer is 8.

Given:

\n\n

In a triangle XYZ, C is circumcentre of this triangle.

\n\n

∠Y = 50 degree

\n\n

∠Z = 70 degree

\n\n

Concept Used:

\n\n

If O is the circumcenter of the △ABC then the angle made at the circumcenter by joining any two vertices of the triangle is twice the angle at the third vertex of the triangle i.e.

\n\n

the angle made at the circumference at the circle.

\n\n

Angle Sum Property: Sum of all the angles in a triangle is 180° .

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

\n\n&", "hn": "\n

दिया गया है:

\n\n

एक त्रिभुज XYZ में, C इस त्रिभुज का परिकेन्द्र है।

\n\n

∠Y = 50 डिग्री

\n\n

∠Z = 70 डिग्री

\n\n

प्रयुक्त अवधारणा:

\n\n

यदि △ABC का परिकेन्द्र O है, तो त्रिभुज के किन्हीं दो शीर्षों को मिलाकर परिकेन्द्र पर बना कोण, त्रिभुज के तीसरे शीर्ष पर बने कोण अर्थात वृत्त की परिधि पर बने कोण का दोगुना होता है।

\n\n

कोण योग गुणधर्म: त्रिभुज के सभी कोणों का योग 180° होता है।

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

\n\n

त्रिभुज XYZ में,

\n\n

∠Y = 50° 

\n\n

∠Z = 70° 

\n\n

कोण योग गुणधर्म का उपयोग करने पर,

\n\n

∠X = 180 - (50 + 70)

\n\n

∠X = 60° 

\n\n

यहाँ, C परिकेन्द्र है। 

\n\n

∠YCZ = 2 × ∠X

\n\n

∠YCZ = 2 × 60

\n\n

∠YCZ = 120° 

\n\n

∴ विकल्प 4 सही उत्तर है।

\nThe area of ABM is 19 cm2

\nConcept used:-
\n
\nThe area of two triangles is directly proportional to
\ntheir base if they have the same height.
\n
\nCalculations:- 
\n
\nArea of the ∆ABM = (1/2) × (BM × AM)

Given :

\n\n

M is the midpoint of the triangle ABC 

\n\n

Concept used :

\n\n

The area of two triangles is directly proportional to their base if they have the same height. 

\n\n

Calculations :

\n\n

\n\n

Area of the ∆ABM = (1/2) × (BM × AM) 

\n\n

⇒ (1/2) × (BM × AM) = 18 

\n\n

⇒ BM × AM = 36

\n\n

Now

\n\n

Area of ∆ABC = (1/2) × (BC × AM) 

\n\n

⇒ (1/2) × (2 × BM × AM)   (BC = 2 BM because M is the mid point) 

\n\n

⇒ BM × AM     

\n\n

⇒ 36 

\n\n

∴ The area of the triangle ABC is 36 cm2

Given:

\n\n

The corresponding angles of two triangles are equal and satisfy \\(\\frac{{PX}}{{ER}}\\, = \\,\\frac{{ZX}}{{RF}}\\, = \\,\\frac{{PZ}}{{EF}}\\)

\n\n

Concept used:

\n\n

If corresponding angles of two triangles are equal then they are said to be similar triangles.

\n\n

Calculations:

\n\n

If corresponding angles of two triangles are equal then they are said to be similar triangles.

\n\n

Let us find the respective corresponding equal angles of the two triangles.

\n\n

According to question, we have :

\n\n

PX and ER are corresponding sides,

\n\n

ZX and RF are corresponding sides

\n\n

and PZ and EF are corresponding sides

\n\n

Hence, angle P and E are corresponding angles, 

\n\n

angle X and angle R are the corresponding angles and angle Z and angle F are the corresponding angles.

\n\n

Hence, this concludes that ∆PXZ is similar to ∆ERF.

दिले:

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

এবং PZ এবং EF সংশ্লিষ্ট পক্ষ

Given:

\n\n

ΔABC is similar to ΔPQR

\n\n

Area of ΔABC is 64 cm2

\n\n

Area of ΔPQR is 121 cm2

\n\n

QR = 15.4 cm 

\n\n

Concept: 

\n\n

The ratio of the areas of two similar triangles is equal to the ratio of the squares of the corresponding sides & medians. 

\n\n

Calculations:

\n\n

According to concept:

\n\n

area(ΔABC)/area(ΔPQR) = BC2/QR2

\n\n

⇒ 64/121 = BC2/QR2 

\n\n

⇒ √(64/121) = BC/QR

\n\n

⇒ 8/11 = BC/15.4

\n\n

⇒BC = (15.4 × 8)/11

\n\n

⇒BC = 11.2

\n\n

∴ BC = 11.2 cm 

", "hn": "\n

\n\n

Δ ABC का क्षेत्रफल 64 सेमी2 .हैవిషయాలు:

\n\n

ΔABC మరియు ΔPQR సరూప త్రిభుజాలు

\n\n

ΔABC వైశాల్యం 64 cm2

\n\n

ΔPQR వైశాల్యం 121 cm2

\n\n

QR = 15.4 cm

\n\n

సిద్ధాంతం:

\n\n

రెండు సరూప త్రిభుజాల వైశాల్యాల నిష్పత్తి వాటి అనురూప భుజాల వర్గాల నిష్పత్తికి సమానం.

\n\n

లెక్కలు:

\n\n

", "mr": "\n

दिलेल्याप्रमाणे:

\n\n

Δ ABC हे Δ PQR याच्या समान आहे.

\n\n

ΔABC चे क्षेत्रफळ 64 सेमी2 आहे

\n\n

ΔPQR चे क्षेत्रफळ 121 सेमी2 आहे

\n\n

QR = 15.4 सेमी

\n\n

संकल्पना:

\n\n

दोन समान त्रिकोणांच्या क्षेत्रांचे गुणोत्तर हे संबंधित बाजू आणि मध्यकाच्या वर्गांच्या गुणोत्तरासमान आहे.

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

संकल्पनेनुसार:

\n\n

(ΔABC)क्षेत्रफळ/(ΔPQR)क्षेत्रफळ = BC2 /QR2

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

⇒ 8/11 = BC/15.4

\n\n

⇒BC = (15.4 × 8)/11

\n\n

⇒BC = 11.2

\n\n

∴ BC = 11.2 सेमी

Given:

\n\n

The areas of two similar triangles ABC and PQR are 64cm2 and 144cm2, respectively.

\n\n

The greatest side of the smaller ΔABC is 24 cm

\n\n

Concept used:

\n\n

The similarity of triangles = If two triangles are similar, then their corresponding angles are congruent and corresponding sides are in equal in proportion 

\n\n

Ratio of area of similar  triangles  = (ratio of sides of the triangles)2 

\n\n

Calculations:

\n\n

Let the greatest side of the bigger ΔPQR be x

\n\n

Greatest side of the smaller \\(\\triangle\\) ABC = 24cm 

\n\n

According to the question,

\n\n

Ratio of the area of triangles = 64 : 144 

\n\n

As, we know that

\n\n

Ratio of the area of triangles = (ratio of sides of the triangles)2

\n\n

⇒ 64/144 = (24/x)2

\n\n

⇒ 8/12 = 24/x

\n\n

⇒ x = 36 cm 

\n\n

∴ The greatest side of the bigger ΔPQR is 36 cm.

\n\n

\n\n

Given

\n\n

AC = (5AB - 4) cm

\n\n

\n\n

AB + BC + AC = 90 cm

\n\n

Formula used

\n\n

Area of triangle = √{s(s -a) (s -b) (s - c)}

\n\n

where s is semi perimeter of triangle = (a + b + c)/2

\n\n

a, b, and c are sides of triangle

\n\n

Calculation

\n\n

let the side AB is x cm

\n\n

AB + BC + AC = 90

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र

\n\n

त्रिभुज का क्षेत्रफल = √{s(s -a) (s -b) (s - c)}

\n\n

जहाँ s त्रिभुज का अर्ध परिमाप है = (a + b + c)/2

\n\n

a, b, और c त्रिभुज की भुजाएं हैं।

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

An angle bisector of a triangle divides the interior angle's opposite side into two segments that are proportional to the other two sides of the triangle.

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

AB/AC = BP/PC

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

⇒ ∠R = 180 - (∠P + ∠Q) = 180 - 120 = 60°

\n\n

∠S = 70°      [∵ ∠P = 70° ]

\n\n

∴ The option 1 is NOT correct.

\n\n

 \\(\\triangle \\)TUV,  \\(\\triangle \\)PQR के समरूप है और क्षेत्रफल 18 cm2 और 32 cm2 है, TU = 6 cm,

प्रयुक्त संकल्पना:

\n\n

इस प्रकार के प्रश्न में, यदि दो त्रिभुज समरूप हैं तो उनके संगत कोण सर्वांगसम होते हैं और संगत क्षेत्रफल उनकी संबंधित भुजाओं के वर्ग के अनुपात में होते हैं।

Given:
\n\n

In ΔXYZ, I is the incentre.

\n\n

∠XYZ = 40

\n\n

Concepts used: 

\n\n

Angle formed at incentre opposite to given side = 90° +( ½ × angle opposite to given side)

\n\n

Calculation:
\n
\nIn ΔXYZ, I is the incentre

\n\n

∠XIZ = 90° + ( ½ × ∠XYZ)

\n\n

\n\n

⇒ ∠XIZ = 90° + 20°

\n\n

⇒ ∠XIZ = 110° 

\n\n

∴ Measure of ∠XIZ is equal to 110°.

\n\n

∴ Option 2 is the correct answer.

\n\n

PR : RQ = 3 : 5

\n\n

QR = 20 cm

\n\n

Concept used:

\n\n

In a ΔABC, if P and Q are the mid-points of AB and AC respectively, then 

\n\n

\n\n

PQ||BC

\n\n

ΔAPQ ~ ΔABC

\n\n

PQ = \\(1\\over2\\) BC

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

दिया गया है:

\n\n

PR : RQ = 3 : 5

\n\n

QR = 20 सेमी

\n\n

प्रयुक्त अवधारणा:

\n\n

एक ΔABC में, यदि P और Q क्रमशः AB और AC के मध्य-बिंदु हैं, तो

\n\n

\n\n

PQ||BC

\n\n

ΔAPQ ~ ΔABC

\n\n

PQ = \\(1\\over2\\) BC

\n\n

\n\n

PR : RQ = 3 : 5

QR = 20 సెం.మీ

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

లెక్కింపు:

\n\n

\n\n

PQ/BC = 1/2

\n\n

QR = 20 సెం.మీ

\n\n

PR = 12 సెం.మీ

\n\n

PQ = 32 cm[PQ = PR + QR]

\n\n

BC = 64 సెం.మీ

\n\n

∴ BC పొడవు 64 సెం.మీ

PR : RQ = 3 : 5

\n\n

x × \\(5\\over8\\) = 20

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

ΔBO ನಲ್ಲಿ

\n\n

∠BOC + ∠OBC + ∠OCB = 180 [OB ಮತ್ತು OC ಎರಡು ದ್ವಿಭಾಜಕಗಳು ∠B ಮತ್ತು ∠C ಕ್ರಮವಾಗಿ]

\n\n

⇒ ∠BOC + 1/2∠B + 1/2∠C = 180

\n\n

⇒ ∠BOC = 180 – 1/2(∠B + ∠C)

\n\n

⇒ ∠BOC = 180 – 1/2(180 – ∠A)

\n\n

⇒ ∠BOC = 180 – 1/2(180 – 50)

\n\n

⇒ ∠BOC = 180 – 1/2(130)

\n\n

⇒ ∠BOC = 180 – 65

\n\n

⇒ ∠BOC = 115

\n\n

∴ ∠BOC ಯ ಅಗತ್ಯವಿರುವ ಮೌಲ್ಯವು 115° ಆಗಿದೆ​

\n\n

Shortcut Trick ⇒ ∠BOC = 90 + A / 2

\n\n

⇒ 90 + 25 = 115°

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

Two triangles are said to be congruent if their sides have the same length and their angles have the same measure. Thus, two triangles can be superimposed side to side and angle to angle.

\n\n

\n\n

AB = DF = 5 cm

\n\n

∠A = ∠F = 80°

\n\n

∠B = ∠D = 40°

\n\n

∠C = ∠E = 180° - 80° - 40° = 60°

\n\n

∴ The required answer is Option 4.

Given:

\n\n

ΔABC ~ ΔPQR and \\(\\rm \\frac{ar(\\Delta ABC)}{ar (\\Delta PQR)} = \\frac{144}{49}\\) 

\n\n

 AB = 12 cm, BC = 7 cm,  AC = 9 cm

\n\n

Concept:

\n\n

\n\n

The ratio of the areas of two similar triangles is equal to the ratio of the squares of the corresponding sides.

\n\n

\\(\\text{Area of (ΔABC)}\\over{\\text{Area of (ΔPQR)}}\\) = \\(({{AC}\\over{PR}})^2\\)

\n\n<", "hn": "\n

दिया गया है:

\n\n

ΔABC ~ ΔPQR और \\(\\rm \\frac{ar(\\Delta ABC)}{ar (\\Delta PQR)} = \\frac{144}{49}\\) 

\n\n

 AB = 12 सेमी, BC = 7 सेमी,  AC = 9 सेमी

\n\n

अवधारणा:

\n\n

\n\n

दो समरूप त्रिभुजों के क्षेत्रफलों का अनुपात संगत भुजाओं के वर्गों के अनुपात के बराबर होता है।

\n\n

\\(\\text{Area of (ΔABC)}\\over{\\text{Area of (ΔPQR)}}\\) = \\(({{AC}\\over{PR}})^2\\)

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

\\(\\text{Area of (ΔABC)}\\over{\\text{Area of (ΔPQR)}}\\) = \\(({{AC}\\over{PR}})^2\\) 

\n\n

⇒ \\(144\\over49\\) = \\(({{AC}\\over{PR}})^2\\) 

\n\n

⇒ \\(AC\\over PR\\) = \\(\\sqrt{144\\over49} \\) = \\(12\\over7\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(9\\over PR\\) = \\(12\\over7\\)

\n\n

⇒ PR = \\({9\\times 7}\\over12\\) = \\(21\\over4\\) सेमी

\n\n

∴ PR = \\(21\\over4\\) सेमी

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

Given: AB = PQ = 7 m, BC = QR = 8 m, AC = PR = 9 m

\n\n

All three corresponding sides are equal.

\n\n

⇒ ∆ABC ≅ ∆PQR (by SSS congruence)

\n\n

∴ The correct answer is option (2).

Given:

\n\n

In ΔSTU, X and Y are the points on sides ST and SU respectively.

\n\n

XY is parallel to TU. If SX ∶ XT = 2 ∶ 5 and UY = 20 cm

\n\n

Concept used:

\n\n

Thales' Theorem states if a line is drawn parallel to one side of a triangle intersecting the other two sides in distinct points, then the other two sides are divided in the same ratio.

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

\n\n

According to the concept,

\n\n

SX ∶ XT = 2 ∶ 5

\n\n

⇒ SY : YU = 2 : 5

\n\n

So, SY = \\(\\frac {20}{5} \\times 2\\) = 8 cm

\n\n

Now, the length of SU

\n\n

⇒ SY + YU

\n\n

⇒ 20 + 8

\n\n

⇒ 28 cm

\n\n

∴ The value of SU is 28 cm.

\n\n73:Td5", "hn": "\n

दिया गया है:

\n\n

ΔSTU में, X और Y भुजाओं ST और SU पर क्रमशः बिंदु हैं।

\n\n

XY, TU के समांतर है।

\n\n

SX ∶ XT = 2 ∶ 5 और UY = 20 सेमी

\n\n

प्रयुक्त अवधारणा:

\n\n

थेल्स के प्रमेय के अनुसार, यदि एक त्रिभुज की एक भुजा के समानांतर एक रेखा खींची जाती है तो वह अन्य दो भुजाओं को अलग-अलग बिंदुओं पर काटती है और उनको समान अनुपात में विभाजित करती है। 

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

\n\n

अवधारणा के अनुसार,

\n\n

SX ∶ XT = 2 ∶ 5

\n\n

⇒ SY : YU = 2 : 5

\n\n

इसलिए, SY = \\(\\frac {20}{5} \\times 2\\) = 8 सेमी

\n\n

अब, SU की लंबाई

\n\n

⇒ SY + YU

\n\n

⇒ 20 + 8

\n\n

⇒ 28 सेमी

\n\n

∴ SU की लंबाई 28 सेमी है।

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

ΔSTUలో, X మరియు Y వరుసగా ST మరియు SU వైపులా ఉండే బిందువులు.

\n\n

XY అనేది TUకి సమాంతరంగా ఉంటుంది. SX ∶ XT = 2 ∶ 5 మరియు UY = 20 సెం.మీ
